- _type_layout_string 5UIKit21SheetLayoutAttributes33_A96866C2920FEF12151F6D0347E0D9CELLC16IsDimmingEnabledV
CStrings:
+ "\t\tEating current touch for window matching predicate: %{public}@; touch: %{public}@"
+ "\t\tEating current touch for window: %{public}@"
+ "\t\tEating touch in %{public}@; animation: %{public}@; animationDelegate does not allow interaction: %{public}@"
+ "\n\n\nThe attachments contain extremely detailed data, including per-keystroke timing, candidate lists and scores,and learned-word data, about what you recently typed. Please review.\n----- begin included text -----\n%@\n----- end included text -----\n"
+ "\n\nAutocorrected from: %@\nAutocorrected to: %@\nIntended text: <provide your intended text here>"
+ "#\""
+ "%2B"
+ "%@ became hidden"
+ "%@ became visible"
+ "%@^%@^%@^%d^%d"
+ "%s svc = %p; permittedPrimaryEdges = %lu"
+ "%s: Invalid attempt to re-register with hingeStateClientComponent: %@; interaction: %@"
+ "%{public}@ Called into an unapproved SPI."
+ "%{public}@ started holding the system gesture gate"
+ "%{public}@ stopped holding the system gesture gate"
